# no-resource-duplicates assessment

## Scope and result

Assessed upstream [Azure/azure-rest-api-specs main at 6e858cc12c77](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/commit/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9), captured on 2026-11-17. This report is an inventory for later remediation decisions. The assessment itself changed no service sources, generated Swagger, suppressions or checked-in dependencies; the fixes are in the separate draft PRs listed below.

**16 distinct violations across 9 TypeSpec service projects.** All 16 have suggested fixes, all verified locally:

- 10 replace legacy interface templates with simpler operation templates.
- 5 use a resource-name override, with the reason given. #2, #4 and #11 moved to overrides after CI showed the simpler templates changed parent-key parameter docs, patterns or locations.
- 1 (#7) merges a version-split resource model by versioning its `properties` envelope.

Each project now has a draft fix PR; see [Fix pull requests](#fix-pull-requests). 0 findings were hidden by source-level suppression. A separate conditional finding from dormant NetApp source, which isn't part of the spec project, is not included in these totals and needs no action. The coverage ledger accounts for 670 normal candidate entrypoints, one supplemental fragment check and 615 distinct declared services reached by successful compilations.

| [app/resource-manager/Microsoft.App/ContainerApps](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/app/resource-manager/Microsoft.App/ContainerApps/main.tsp)                                                                                   |          3 |

## Method and interpretation

- **Released tooling:** ARM library `@azure-tools/typespec-azure-resource-manager@0.74.0`, compiler `@typespec/compiler@1.18.0`, and the snapshot's frozen `pnpm-lock.yaml` (pnpm 12.6.0; Node 26.13.0). See the [rule documentation](https://azure.github.io/typespec-azure/docs/libraries/azure-resource-manager/rules/no-resource-duplicates) and [release change](https://github.com/Azure/typespec-azure/pull/5974).
- **Discovery:** enumerated every tracked `specification/**/tspconfig.*` project, every `main.tsp`, and client-only project entrypoints. Independently parsed all tracked TypeSpec source candidates for namespace `@service` and `@armProviderNamespace` declarations, then reconciled their files with successfully compiled source graphs. Shared libraries and incomplete imported fragments are accounted for separately, not silently reported as clean services. The one unreferenced provider-declaration file received a clearly separated supplemental check, described below.
- **Unversioned graph only:** resolved each entrypoint's compiler configuration and compiled with emission disabled, no emitters and no version projection. Evaluated the released rule's own root listener on that program; its no-options `resolveArmResources(program)` call sees the multi-version declaration graph, not a separately projected graph for each API version. A version-transition collision is therefore not proof of a duplicate in any individual API version.
- **Explicit evaluation:** the rule is disabled in the central ARM ruleset in this release. Other configured lint rules were disabled for this focused assessment; compiler/type-checker and library validation errors were still retained. The published rule listener was invoked directly before suppression, using the compiler's project-source diagnostic filter. Every finding was also passed through `program.reportDiagnostic` to identify source-suppressed findings without editing sources. Its output and suppression handling were checked against normal explicit linter enablement.
- **Identity:** the rule compares ARM resource type and full instance path case-insensitively, retaining parameter names. It reports subsequent logical records with an authored GET or PUT identity operation; synthetic ancestors without their own identity operations are excluded. Different scopes, parent paths, extension targets, singleton names and differently named path parameters are not flattened together.
- **Counting:** 16 baseline diagnostic occurrences were deduplicated to 16 findings by diagnostic source location, both logical names, resource type and full instance path. The supplemental graph reported 1 additional conditional finding. Each item lists the diagnostic's source plus the colliding records' associated operation sources; one finding can require changing more than the operation named by the warning.
- **Fix status:** fixes prefer removing `Legacy.LegacyOperations`/`Legacy.ExtensionOperations` interface templates where they are used at the resource's standard `@autoRoute` path. Those templates exist for resources exposed at additional instance paths, and by design derive a different logical resource name, so mixing them with standard templates at the same path causes the duplicate. Standard operation templates (with `Error`/`Response`/`BaseParameters` parameters) or Legacy _operation_ templates (`Legacy.CreateOrReplaceSync/Async`, `Legacy.CustomPatchSync/Async`, `Legacy.Extension.*`, which support `OptionalRequestBody` and custom request models) produce the same HTTP API with less complexity. Upstream fixes [#48177](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/pull/48177) and [#48277](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/pull/48277) used this pattern. The `ResourceName`/`OverrideResourceName` override is the fallback, and is preferred only where a finding explains why. Every fix was prototyped locally against the snapshot: the rule was enabled, the project recompiled and re-emitted, and the Swagger diff inspected. Swagger was unchanged or changed only in documentation (parameter descriptions/patterns now matching the resource's other operations); no wire-contract changes. CI's BreakingChange check treats an added parameter `pattern` or a changed parameter location as breaking. If the standard templates change a parent key that is copied from the resource's keys, `@@doc`/`@@pattern` can't target it (`invalid-ref`), so those findings use the name override. Snippets show only the changed operations; `/* existing ... */` marks arguments to keep as-is. Prototypes were reverted.

## Violations

### apimanagement/resource-manager/Microsoft.ApiManagement/ApiManagement

#### 1. API Management private endpoint connection PUT is identified as its parent service

The standalone Legacy.CreateOrUpdateAsync PUT uses ApiManagementServiceResource as its resource identity and appends the private-endpoint name through Parameters. GET and DELETE use `PrivateEndpoints<PrivateEndpointConnection>`, producing a separate child-resource record at the identical HTTP instance path.

**Collision:** `ApiManagementServiceResourcePrivateEndpointConnection` versus `ApiManagementServiceResource`. **ARM type:** `Microsoft.ApiManagement/service/privateEndpointConnections`.

**Instance path:** `/subscriptions/{subscriptionId}/resourceGroups/{resourceGroupName}/providers/Microsoft.ApiManagement/service/{serviceName}/privateEndpointConnections/{privateEndpointConnectionName}`.

**Diagnostic source:** [`Microsoft.ApiManagement.PrivateEndpointConnections.getByName`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/apimanagement/resource-manager/Microsoft.ApiManagement/ApiManagement/PrivateEndpointConnection.tsp#L23).

| Logical record                                          | Associated authored operations                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ApiManagementServiceResource`                          | [`Microsoft.ApiManagement.PrivateEndpointConnections.createOrUpdate`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/apimanagement/resource-manager/Microsoft.ApiManagement/ApiManagement/PrivateEndpointConnection.tsp#L29)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ApiManagementServiceResourcePrivateEndpointConnection` | [`Microsoft.ApiManagement.PrivateEndpointConnections.getByName`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/apimanagement/resource-manager/Microsoft.ApiManagement/ApiManagement/PrivateEndpointConnection.tsp#L23); [`Microsoft.ApiManagement.PrivateEndpointConnections.delete`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/apimanagement/resource-manager/Microsoft.ApiManagement/ApiManagement/PrivateEndpointConnection.tsp#L50); [`Microsoft.ApiManagement.PrivateEndpointConnections.listByService`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/apimanagement/resource-manager/Microsoft.ApiManagement/ApiManagement/PrivateEndpointConnection.tsp#L61) |

**Suggested fix:** Keep a legacy _interface_ template here: declare a `Legacy.LegacyOperations` alias for the service-instance parent and `privateEndpointConnectionName` key, set `ResourceName = "ApiManagementServiceResourcePrivateEndpointConnection"`, and route the PUT through its `CreateOrUpdateAsync` with `PrivateEndpointConnection` as the resource while keeping `Request = PrivateEndpointConnectionRequest` and the existing response.

```tsp
alias PrivateEndpointConnectionLegacyOps = Azure.ResourceManager.Legacy.LegacyOperations<
  {
    ...ApiVersionParameter;
    ...SubscriptionIdParameter;
    ...ResourceGroupParameter;
    ...Azure.ResourceManager.Legacy.Provider;

    /** The name of the API Management service. */
    @path @segment("service") @key
    @pattern("^[a-zA-Z](?:[a-zA-Z0-9-]*[a-zA-Z0-9])?$") @minLength(1) @maxLength(50)
    serviceName: string;
  },
  {
    /** API revision identifier. ... */
    @path @segment("privateEndpointConnections") @key
    privateEndpointConnectionName: string;
  },
  ResourceName = "ApiManagementServiceResourcePrivateEndpointConnection"
>;

// in interface PrivateEndpointConnections
createOrUpdate is PrivateEndpointConnectionLegacyOps.CreateOrUpdateAsync<
  PrivateEndpointConnection,
  Request = PrivateEndpointConnectionRequest,
  Response = /* existing ArmResourceUpdatedResponse | ArmAcceptedLroResponse union */
>;
```

Prototyped: rule clears; emitted Swagger unchanged.

**Why the name override is preferred here:** the simpler alternatives were prototyped and rejected. `PrivateEndpointOperations.CreateOrReplaceAsync<ApiManagementServiceResource>` clears the rule, but the `PrivateEndpoints` templates have no `Request` parameter, so the PUT body changes from `PrivateEndpointConnectionRequest` to the common-types `PrivateEndpointConnection` (a wire-contract change). `Legacy.CreateOrReplaceAsync<PrivateEndpointConnection, PrivateEndpointConnectionRequest, BaseParameters = ...>` cannot place the service parent before the provider segment (`PrivateEndpointConnectionResource` has no `@parentResource`), so it fails with a duplicate `provider` parameter. The interface template is the only option that preserves both the route and the request model.

**Assessment:** Name override via interface template (verified, contract preserving).

### app/resource-manager/Microsoft.App/ContainerApps

#### 2. Connected-environment Dapr component mixes standard PUT and legacy GET/DELETE

The standard PUT identifies DaprComponent, while the connected-environment legacy GET and DELETE identify ConnectedEnvironmentsDaprComponents. These are the same connected-environment instance, not the separate managed-environment Dapr route elsewhere in this file.

**Collision:** `DaprComponent` versus `ConnectedEnvironmentsDaprComponents`. **ARM type:** `Microsoft.App/connectedEnvironments/daprComponents`.

**Instance path:** `/subscriptions/{subscriptionId}/resourceGroups/{resourceGroupName}/providers/Microsoft.App/connectedEnvironments/{connectedEnvironmentName}/daprComponents/{componentName}`.

**Diagnostic source:** [`Microsoft.App.ConnectedEnvironmentsDaprComponents.createOrUpdate`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/app/resource-manager/Microsoft.App/ContainerApps/DaprComponent.tsp#L87).

| Logical record                        | Associated authored operations                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ConnectedEnvironmentsDaprComponents` | [`Microsoft.App.ConnectedEnvironmentsDaprComponents.get`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/app/resource-manager/Microsoft.App/ContainerApps/DaprComponent.tsp#L81); [`Microsoft.App.ConnectedEnvironmentsDaprComponents.delete`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/app/resource-manager/Microsoft.App/ContainerApps/DaprComponent.tsp#L93); [`Microsoft.App.ConnectedEnvironmentsDaprComponents.list`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/app/resource-manager/Microsoft.App/ContainerApps/DaprComponent.tsp#L99); [`Microsoft.App.ConnectedEnvironmentsDaprComponents.listSecrets`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/app/resource-manager/Microsoft.App/ContainerApps/DaprComponent.tsp#L108) |
| `DaprComponent`                       | [`Microsoft.App.ConnectedEnvironmentsDaprComponents.createOrUpdate`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/app/resource-manager/Microsoft.App/ContainerApps/DaprComponent.tsp#L87); [`Microsoft.App.ConnectedEnvironmentsDaprComponents.list`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/app/resource-manager/Microsoft.App/ContainerApps/DaprComponent.tsp#L99); [`Microsoft.App.ConnectedEnvironmentsDaprComponents.listSecrets`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/app/resource-manager/Microsoft.App/ContainerApps/DaprComponent.tsp#L108)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

**Suggested fix (applied in #48355):** Set `ResourceName = "DaprComponent"` on both connected-environment aliases, `DaprComponentOps` and `DaprComponentCustomErrorOps`. Rule clears; Swagger is unchanged.

```tsp
alias DaprComponentOps = Azure.ResourceManager.Legacy.LegacyOperations<
  { /* existing parent parameters */ },
  { /* existing componentName key */ },
  ResourceName = "DaprComponent"
>;
```

**Why not the simpler templates:** the standard templates take the parent key `connectedEnvironmentName` from the resource's `KeysOf`/`ParentKeysOf`, so its Swagger description changes. LintDiff and BreakingChange both failed CI on that change. Those keys are copied by a decorator, so `@@doc(Op::parameters.connectedEnvironmentName, ...)` fails with `invalid-ref`. An explicit `BaseParameters` model fails with `duplicate-property`. The original documentation can't be restored with the standard templates.

**Alternative (rejected):** remove the aliases and use standard operation templates with an `Error` parameter.

```tsp
get is ArmResourceRead<DaprComponent, Error = DefaultErrorResponse>;
delete is ArmResourceDeleteWithoutOkAsync<DaprComponent>;
list is ArmResourceListByParent<
  DaprComponent,
  Response = ArmResponse<DaprComponentsCollection>,
  Error = DefaultErrorResponse
>;
listSecrets is ArmResourceActionSync<
  DaprComponent,
  void,
  ArmResponse<DaprSecretsCollection>,
  Error = DefaultErrorResponse
>;
```

Keep the managed-environment `DaprComponentOperationGroupOps`, which is on a different path.

With the alternative, the rule clears, but `connectedEnvironmentName` gets the description "Name of the connectedEnvironment.", which fails CI.

**Assessment:** Resource name override (verified in #48355; Swagger unchanged).

#### 3. Logic app extension legacy PUTs derive a plural logical name

Standard GET/DELETE resolve LogicApp, but the two version-gated legacy PUT declarations resolve LogicApps at the same container-app extension path.

**Collision:** `LogicApps` versus `LogicApp`. **ARM type:** `Microsoft.App/logicApps`.

**Instance path:** `/subscriptions/{subscriptionId}/resourceGroups/{resourceGroupName}/providers/Microsoft.App/containerApps/{containerAppName}/providers/Microsoft.App/logicApps/{logicAppName}`.

**Diagnostic source:** [`Microsoft.App.LogicApps.createOrUpdate`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/app/resource-manager/Microsoft.App/ContainerApps/LogicApp.tsp#L71).

| Logical record | Associated authored operations                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| -------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `LogicApp`     | [`Microsoft.App.LogicApps.get`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/app/resource-manager/Microsoft.App/ContainerApps/LogicApp.tsp#L59); [`Microsoft.App.LogicApps.delete`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/app/resource-manager/Microsoft.App/ContainerApps/LogicApp.tsp#L89); [`Microsoft.App.LogicApps.deployWorkflowArtifacts`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/app/resource-manager/Microsoft.App/ContainerApps/LogicApp.tsp#L101); [`Microsoft.App.LogicApps.listWorkflowsConnections`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/app/resource-manager/Microsoft.App/ContainerApps/LogicApp.tsp#L114); [`Microsoft.App.LogicApps.invoke`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/app/resource-manager/Microsoft.App/ContainerApps/LogicApp.tsp#L129)                          |
| `LogicApps`    | [`Microsoft.App.LogicApps.createOrUpdate`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/app/resource-manager/Microsoft.App/ContainerApps/LogicApp.tsp#L71); [`Microsoft.App.LogicApps.createOrUpdateStable`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/app/resource-manager/Microsoft.App/ContainerApps/LogicApp.tsp#L78); [`Microsoft.App.LogicApps.deployWorkflowArtifacts`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/app/resource-manager/Microsoft.App/ContainerApps/LogicApp.tsp#L101); [`Microsoft.App.LogicApps.listWorkflowsConnections`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/app/resource-manager/Microsoft.App/ContainerApps/LogicApp.tsp#L114); [`Microsoft.App.LogicApps.invoke`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/app/resource-manager/Microsoft.App/ContainerApps/LogicApp.tsp#L129) |

**Suggested fix:** Remove `LogicAppOps` and express both version-specific PUTs as `Legacy.CreateOrReplaceSync` operation templates. `LogicAppParentParameter` is passed as `BaseParameters`, so the extension route is unchanged.

```tsp
@sharedRoute
@removed(Versions.v2026_01_01)
@removed(Versions.v2026_07_01)
createOrUpdate is Azure.ResourceManager.Legacy.CreateOrReplaceSync<
  LogicApp,
  BaseParameters = LogicAppParentParameter,
  Error = DefaultErrorResponse
>;

@sharedRoute
@added(Versions.v2026_01_01)
createOrUpdateStable is Azure.ResourceManager.Legacy.CreateOrReplaceSync<
  LogicApp,
  BaseParameters = LogicAppParentParameter,
  Response = ArmResourceUpdatedResponse<LogicApp> | ArmResourceCreatedSyncResponse<LogicApp>,
  Error = DefaultErrorResponse,
  OptionalRequestBody = true
>;
```

Prototyped: rule clears; LogicApp Swagger unchanged.

**Fallback:** set `ResourceName = "LogicApp"` on `LogicAppOps`.

**Assessment:** Simpler Legacy operation template (verified).

#### 4. Managed certificate legacy PUT is split from standard GET/PATCH/DELETE

ManagedCertificateOps derives ManagedEnvironmentsManagedCertificates for PUT, while the standard operations use the model name ManagedCertificate at the same managed-environment certificate path.

**Collision:** `ManagedEnvironmentsManagedCertificates` versus `ManagedCertificate`. **ARM type:** `Microsoft.App/managedEnvironments/managedCertificates`.

**Instance path:** `/subscriptions/{subscriptionId}/resourceGroups/{resourceGroupName}/providers/Microsoft.App/managedEnvironments/{environmentName}/managedCertificates/{managedCertificateName}`.

**Diagnostic source:** [`Microsoft.App.ManagedCertificates.createOrUpdate`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/app/resource-manager/Microsoft.App/ContainerApps/ManagedCertificate.tsp#L65).

| Logical record                           | Associated authored operations                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ---------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ManagedCertificate`                     | [`Microsoft.App.ManagedCertificates.get`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/app/resource-manager/Microsoft.App/ContainerApps/ManagedCertificate.tsp#L58); [`Microsoft.App.ManagedCertificates.update`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/app/resource-manager/Microsoft.App/ContainerApps/ManagedCertificate.tsp#L83); [`Microsoft.App.ManagedCertificates.delete`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/app/resource-manager/Microsoft.App/ContainerApps/ManagedCertificate.tsp#L92); [`Microsoft.App.ManagedCertificates.list`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/app/resource-manager/Microsoft.App/ContainerApps/ManagedCertificate.tsp#L98) |
| `ManagedEnvironmentsManagedCertificates` | [`Microsoft.App.ManagedCertificates.createOrUpdate`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/app/resource-manager/Microsoft.App/ContainerApps/ManagedCertificate.tsp#L65); [`Microsoft.App.ManagedCertificates.list`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/app/resource-manager/Microsoft.App/ContainerApps/ManagedCertificate.tsp#L98)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

**Suggested fix (applied in #48355):** Set `ResourceName = "ManagedCertificate"` on `ManagedCertificateOps`. Rule clears; Swagger is unchanged.

**Why not the simpler template:** `Legacy.CreateOrReplaceAsync` takes `environmentName` from the parent `ManagedEnvironment` key. That adds the pattern `^[-\w\._\(\)]+$` to the PUT parameter, which BreakingChange reports as ConstraintChanged, and changes its description. As with #2, the copied parent key can't be targeted by `@@doc`/`@@pattern`, and an explicit `BaseParameters` model fails with `duplicate-property`.

**Assessment:** Resource name override (verified in #48355; Swagger unchanged).

### confluent/resource-manager/Microsoft.Confluent/Confluent

#### 5. Confluent organization legacy PUT/PATCH use the collection-derived name

GET/DELETE identify OrganizationResource; PUT/PATCH from OrganizationResourceRoleBindingIdOperationGroup derive Organizations at the same organization instance path.

**Collision:** `Organizations` versus `OrganizationResource`. **ARM type:** `Microsoft.Confluent/organizations`.

**Suggested fix:** Delete the `OrganizationResourceRoleBindingIdOperationGroup` interface (the `LegacyOperations` instantiation used only for the organization PUT/PATCH) and use Legacy operation templates. Keep the separate role-binding operations and actions interfaces.

```tsp
create is Azure.ResourceManager.Legacy.CreateOrReplaceAsync<
  OrganizationResource,
  Error = ResourceProviderDefaultErrorResponse,
  OptionalRequestBody = true
>;
update is Azure.ResourceManager.Legacy.CustomPatchSync<
  OrganizationResource,
  PatchModel = OrganizationResourceUpdate,
  Error = ResourceProviderDefaultErrorResponse,
  OptionalRequestBody = true
>;
```

Prototyped: rule clears; Swagger unchanged.

**Fallback:** set `ResourceName = "OrganizationResource"` on the interface instantiation.

**Assessment:** Simpler Legacy operation templates (verified).

create is Azure.ResourceManager.Legacy.CreateOrReplaceAsync<

>;

  ElasticMonitorResource,

>;

**Fallback:** set `ResourceName = "ElasticMonitorResource"` on both interface instantiations.

**Assessment:** Simpler Legacy operation templates (verified).

[`Microsoft.ManagedNetworkFabric.NetworkFabrics.viewDeviceConfigurationDeprecated`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/managednetworkfabric/resource-manager/Microsoft.ManagedNetworkFabric/ManagedNetworkFabric/resources/NetworkFabric.tsp#L427); [`Microsoft.ManagedNetworkFabric.NetworkFabrics.armConfigurationDiffDeprecated`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/managednetworkfabric/resource-manager/Microsoft.ManagedNetworkFabric/ManagedNetworkFabric/resources/NetworkFabric.tsp#L449)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

**Suggested fix:** Merge the two models into one resource whose `properties` envelope is versioned. `NetworkFabricDeprecated` exists only because `properties` is client-flattened (`x-ms-client-flatten`) from 2025-07-15 onward but not before, and `@@Legacy.flattenProperty` cannot be versioned. The flatten is applied to `properties`, which `TrackedResource<>` injects. So:

- Declare `NetworkFabric` with a tracked-resource base that has no `properties` property. It is equivalent to `TrackedResource<NetworkFabricProperties, false>`.
  - A user-defined resource _template_ fails, because the ARM library's resource-name check only skips templates in its own namespaces. The base decorators are therefore applied directly to the model, as the ElasticSan `VolumeGroup` spec does.
- Declare the envelope property explicitly, as two versioned properties:
  - `properties`, `@added(v2025_07_15)`, with the unscoped flatten.
  - `flattenedProperties`, `@removed(v2025_07_15) @renamedFrom(v2025_07_15, "properties")`, with the javascript-only flatten.
- Point `back-compatible.tsp` at `NetworkFabric.flattenedProperties` instead of `NetworkFabricDeprecated.properties`.
- Collapse the identical lifecycle operation pairs into unversioned operations.
- Keep versioned `@sharedRoute` pairs, both on `NetworkFabric`, only for actions whose request or response really differs.
- Delete `NetworkFabricDeprecated` and `NetworkFabricsListResultDeprecated`.

```typespec
#suppress "@azure-tools/typespec-azure-core/no-private-usage" "Needed to define a resource whose properties envelope differs by api-version"
#suppress "@azure-tools/typespec-azure-core/composition-over-inheritance" "Equivalent to the standard TrackedResource template"
@doc("The Network Fabric resource definition.")
@Azure.ResourceManager.Private.armResourceInternal(NetworkFabricProperties)
@TypeSpec.Http.Private.includeInapplicableMetadataInPayload(false)
model NetworkFabric extends Azure.ResourceManager.Foundations.TrackedResource {
  @added(Versions.v2025_07_15)
  properties: NetworkFabricProperties;

  @removed(Versions.v2025_07_15)
  @renamedFrom(Versions.v2025_07_15, "properties")
  flattenedProperties: NetworkFabricProperties;

  /* existing name and identity (identity is @added(Versions.v2024_06_15_preview)) */
}

// back-compatible.tsp
@@Legacy.flattenProperty(NetworkFabric.properties);
@@Legacy.flattenProperty(NetworkFabric.flattenedProperties, "javascript");
```

**Assessment:** Fixed in draft PR [#48356](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/pull/48356). The rule reports 0 hits, and generated Swagger is unchanged for all four API versions.

### nginx/resource-manager/Nginx.NginxPlus/NginxPlus

| `ManagementGroupGroupQuotaSubscriptionId` | [`Microsoft.Quota.GroupQuotaSubscriptionIds.get`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/quota/resource-manager/Microsoft.Quota/Quota/GroupQuotaSubscriptionId.tsp#L72); [`Microsoft.Quota.GroupQuotaSubscriptionIds.delete`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/quota/resource-manager/Microsoft.Quota/Quota/GroupQuotaSubscriptionId.tsp#L110); [`Microsoft.Quota.GroupQuotaSubscriptionIds.list`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/quota/resource-manager/Microsoft.Quota/Quota/GroupQuotaSubscriptionId.tsp#L122)           |
| `GroupQuotasSubscriptions`                | [`Microsoft.Quota.GroupQuotaSubscriptionIds.createOrUpdate`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/quota/resource-manager/Microsoft.Quota/Quota/GroupQuotaSubscriptionId.tsp#L81); [`Microsoft.Quota.GroupQuotaSubscriptionIds.update`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/quota/resource-manager/Microsoft.Quota/Quota/GroupQuotaSubscriptionId.tsp#L98); [`Microsoft.Quota.GroupQuotaSubscriptionIds.list`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/quota/resource-manager/Microsoft.Quota/Quota/GroupQuotaSubscriptionId.tsp#L122) |

**Suggested fix (applied in #48363):** Keep `GroupQuotaSubscriptionIdsOps` and add `ResourceName = "ManagementGroupGroupQuotaSubscriptionId"`. This is the name the standard Extension GET/DELETE derive, so the override must match it rather than the model name. Rule clears; Swagger is unchanged.

**Why not the simpler templates:** the `Legacy.Extension` templates make `subscriptionId` (the extension resource key) an inline method parameter, replacing the common client `$ref`. BreakingChange reports this as 4x ParameterLocationHasChanged.

**Alternative (rejected):**

```tsp
createOrUpdate is Azure.ResourceManager.Legacy.Extension.CreateOrReplaceAsync<
  Extension.ManagementGroup<"managementGroupId">,
  GroupQuotaSubscriptionId,
  void,
  Response = /* existing response */
>;
update is Azure.ResourceManager.Legacy.Extension.CustomPatchAsync<
  Extension.ManagementGroup<"managementGroupId">,
  GroupQuotaSubscriptionId,
  PatchModel = void,
  Response = /* existing response */
>;
```

**Assessment:** Resource name override (verified in #48363; Swagger unchanged).

**Why the override is preferred:** the list operation is single-page (C# alone marks it pageable through client.tsp), and there is no `Legacy.Extension` single-page list template. Removing the interface templates would need `Extension.ListByTarget` (pageable) plus replacing `@@markAsPageable(..., "csharp")` with `@@disablePageable(SentinelOnboardingStates.list, "!csharp")`. That was verified to work, but it is more complex than the override. A `Legacy.Extension` single-page list template (for example `Legacy.Extension.ListSinglePageByTarget`) would make the simpler fix possible and is worth requesting in Azure/typespec-azure.

**Assessment:** Name override (verified); simpler template blocked by a library gap.

### storage/resource-manager/Microsoft.Storage/Storage

#### 15. Storage singleton immutability policy legacy PUT uses a different logical name

Standard GET/DELETE resolve ImmutabilityPolicy, while the legacy PUT derives ContainersImmutabilityPolicies at the same blob-container singleton path ending in immutabilityPolicies/default.

**Collision:** `ContainersImmutabilityPolicies` versus `ImmutabilityPolicy`. **ARM type:** `Microsoft.Storage/storageAccounts/blobServices/containers/immutabilityPolicies`.

**Instance path:** `/subscriptions/{subscriptionId}/resourceGroups/{resourceGroupName}/providers/Microsoft.Storage/storageAccounts/{accountName}/blobServices/default/containers/{containerName}/immutabilityPolicies/default`.

**Diagnostic source:** [`Microsoft.Storage.ImmutabilityPolicies.createOrUpdateImmutabilityPolicy`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/storage/resource-manager/Microsoft.Storage/Storage/ImmutabilityPolicy.tsp#L99).

| Logical record                   | Associated authored operations                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| -------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ImmutabilityPolicy`             | [`Microsoft.Storage.ImmutabilityPolicies.getImmutabilityPolicy`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/storage/resource-manager/Microsoft.Storage/Storage/ImmutabilityPolicy.tsp#L81); [`Microsoft.Storage.ImmutabilityPolicies.deleteImmutabilityPolicy`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/storage/resource-manager/Microsoft.Storage/Storage/ImmutabilityPolicy.tsp#L119); [`Microsoft.Storage.ImmutabilityPolicies.lockImmutabilityPolicy`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/storage/resource-manager/Microsoft.Storage/Storage/ImmutabilityPolicy.tsp#L137); [`Microsoft.Storage.ImmutabilityPolicies.extendImmutabilityPolicy`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/storage/resource-manager/Microsoft.Storage/Storage/ImmutabilityPolicy.tsp#L156) |
| `ContainersImmutabilityPolicies` | [`Microsoft.Storage.ImmutabilityPolicies.createOrUpdateImmutabilityPolicy`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/storage/resource-manager/Microsoft.Storage/Storage/ImmutabilityPolicy.tsp#L99); [`Microsoft.Storage.ImmutabilityPolicies.lockImmutabilityPolicy`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/storage/resource-manager/Microsoft.Storage/Storage/ImmutabilityPolicy.tsp#L137); [`Microsoft.Storage.ImmutabilityPolicies.extendImmutabilityPolicy`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/storage/resource-manager/Microsoft.Storage/Storage/ImmutabilityPolicy.tsp#L156)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

**Suggested fix:** Delete `CreateOrUpdateImmutabilityPolicyOps` and use `Legacy.CreateOrReplaceSync`, passing the `If-Match` header through `Parameters`.

```tsp
createOrUpdateImmutabilityPolicy is Azure.ResourceManager.Legacy.CreateOrReplaceSync<
  ImmutabilityPolicy,
  Parameters = {
    /** (existing If-Match doc and #suppress) */
    @header("If-Match")
    `If-Match`?: string;
  },
  Response = ArmResourceUpdatedResponse<ImmutabilityPolicy> & ETagHeader,
  Error = ErrorResponse,
  OptionalRequestBody = true
>;
```

Prototyped with #16: rule clears; Swagger unchanged.

**Fallback:** set `ResourceName = "ImmutabilityPolicy"` on `CreateOrUpdateImmutabilityPolicyOps`.

**Assessment:** Simpler Legacy operation template (verified).

#### 16. Storage table legacy PUT/PATCH derive a collection-chain identity

Standard GET/DELETE identify Table, while TablesCreateOps PUT/PATCH identify TableServicesTables at the same storage-account/tableServices/default/tables instance.

**Collision:** `TableServicesTables` versus `Table`. **ARM type:** `Microsoft.Storage/storageAccounts/tableServices/tables`.

**Instance path:** `/subscriptions/{subscriptionId}/resourceGroups/{resourceGroupName}/providers/Microsoft.Storage/storageAccounts/{accountName}/tableServices/default/tables/{tableName}`.

**Diagnostic source:** [`Microsoft.Storage.Tables.create`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/storage/resource-manager/Microsoft.Storage/Storage/Table.tsp#L77).

| Logical record        | Associated authored operations                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| --------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `Table`               | [`Microsoft.Storage.Tables.get`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/storage/resource-manager/Microsoft.Storage/Storage/Table.tsp#L71); [`Microsoft.Storage.Tables.delete`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/storage/resource-manager/Microsoft.Storage/Storage/Table.tsp#L99); [`Microsoft.Storage.Tables.list`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/storage/resource-manager/Microsoft.Storage/Storage/Table.tsp#L108)    |
| `TableServicesTables` | [`Microsoft.Storage.Tables.create`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/storage/resource-manager/Microsoft.Storage/Storage/Table.tsp#L77); [`Microsoft.Storage.Tables.update`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/storage/resource-manager/Microsoft.Storage/Storage/Table.tsp#L87); [`Microsoft.Storage.Tables.list`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/storage/resource-manager/Microsoft.Storage/Storage/Table.tsp#L108) |

**Suggested fix:** Delete `TablesCreateOps` and use Legacy operation templates.

```tsp
create is Azure.ResourceManager.Legacy.CreateOrReplaceSync<
  Table,
  Response = ArmResourceUpdatedResponse<Table>,
  Error = CloudError,
  OptionalRequestBody = true
>;
@patch(#{ implicitOptionality: false })
update is Azure.ResourceManager.Legacy.CustomPatchSync<
  Table,
  PatchModel = Table,
  Response = ArmResourceUpdatedResponse<Table>,
  Error = CloudError,
  OptionalRequestBody = true
>;
```

Prototyped with #15: rule clears; Swagger unchanged.

**Fallback:** set `ResourceName = "Table"` on `TablesCreateOps`.

**Assessment:** Simpler Legacy operation templates (verified).

## Supplemental dormant-source finding (excluded from baseline totals)

### netapp/resource-manager/Microsoft.NetApp/NetApp/Elastic

#### 17. Supplemental only: dormant NetApp elastic region-info source would duplicate the active singleton

Elastic/ElasticRegionInfoResource.tsp declares the same provider namespace but is not imported by upstream NetApp/main.tsp or any other successful entrypoint. A read-only supplemental graph that adds that import exposes RegionInfoResource and ElasticRegionInfoResource at the same singleton region-info path. The augmented graph also has four duplicate-operation errors and two duplicate-client-name errors; this is conditional, partial evidence, not an additional violation in upstream main's configured service graph.

**Collision:** `RegionInfoResource` versus `ElasticRegionInfoResource`. **ARM type:** `Microsoft.NetApp/locations/regionInfos`.

**Instance path:** `/subscriptions/{subscriptionId}/providers/Microsoft.NetApp/locations/{location}/regionInfos/default`.

**Diagnostic source:** [`Microsoft.NetApp.RegionInfoResources.get`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/netapp/resource-manager/Microsoft.NetApp/NetApp/RegionInfoResource.tsp#L35). **Partial evidence: this program has compilation errors; see coverage gaps.**

| Logical record              | Associated authored operations                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| --------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ElasticRegionInfoResource` | [`Microsoft.NetApp.ElasticRegionInfoResources.get`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/netapp/resource-manager/Microsoft.NetApp/NetApp/Elastic/ElasticRegionInfoResource.tsp#L139); [`Microsoft.NetApp.ElasticRegionInfoResources.list`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/netapp/resource-manager/Microsoft.NetApp/NetApp/Elastic/ElasticRegionInfoResource.tsp#L147) |
| `RegionInfoResource`        | [`Microsoft.NetApp.RegionInfoResources.get`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/netapp/resource-manager/Microsoft.NetApp/NetApp/RegionInfoResource.tsp#L35); [`Microsoft.NetApp.RegionInfoResources.list`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/netapp/resource-manager/Microsoft.NetApp/NetApp/RegionInfoResource.tsp#L43)                                               |

**Suggested fix:** None needed. The file is not imported by the NetApp spec project. If it is activated later, determine whether the dormant elastic file is obsolete, an unfinished replacement or intended to represent a versioned form of RegionInfoResource before activating it. A logical-name override alone would not resolve its duplicate HTTP GET/list routes or the AvailabilityZoneMapping client-name conflicts. Keep this item separate from the 16 findings in actual upstream service entrypoints.

**Assessment:** No action needed. The dormant file is not part of the spec project, so it doesn't affect the configured service.

## Comparison with the Report_20261106 duplicate resource errors

The earlier [TypeSpec ARM resource data report](https://github.com/markcowl/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/arm-resource-data-all-specs/documentation/typespec-arm-resource-data/Report_20261106/README.md#duplicate-resource-errors) lists 31 duplicate groups in 13 projects at [`25e6580`](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/commit/25e6580ed12cabbca57863ab3c2b13b6657dfa0e). It used a pre-release ARM library build (`0.74.0-pr.5976.4439`). All 16 findings in this report match a group in that report. The other 15 groups are accounted for below.

- **Same violation in both reports (16 groups):** API Management private endpoint connections; Container Apps Dapr components, logic apps and managed certificates; Confluent organizations; Elastic monitors; Managed Network Fabric network fabrics; Nginx deployments and WAF policies; Quota group quotas and group quota subscriptions; Security Insights automation rules, entity queries and onboarding states; Storage immutability policies and tables.
- **Fixed upstream between the two snapshots (14 groups):** each was removed by a merged fix, and this assessment found no violation for it at `6e858cc`.

| Earlier group                                                                                            | Fix                                                                               |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| API Management `service/apis/tagDescriptions`, `service/templates`                                       | [#47949](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/pull/47949) (`f5d8b5a7cd`) |
| BotService `botServices/channels`                                                                        | [#48094](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/pull/48094) (`63aae3d37d`) |
| Confluent `organizations/environments`, `.../clusters`, `.../clusters/connectors`, `.../clusters/topics` | [#48095](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/pull/48095) (`7463f27998`) |
| Dashboard `grafana/privateEndpointConnections`                                                           | [#48177](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/pull/48177) (`e10e36a4bc`) |
| Elastic `monitors/monitoredSubscriptions`, `monitors/openAIIntegrations`, `monitors/tagRules`            | [#48183](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/pull/48183) (`fef5254605`) |
| MySQL `flexibleServers/backupsV2`                                                                        | [#48184](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/pull/48184) (`eacc4548e7`) |
| Nginx `nginxDeployments/certificates`, `nginxDeployments/configurations`                                 | [#48277](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/pull/48277) (`3a76c11c29`) |

- **Not a rule violation (1 group): IoTHub `Microsoft.Devices/iotHubs`.** The source is unchanged between snapshots. The earlier report groups records by lowercased type and path with parameter names removed, and it counts every resolved record. One of the two records is `IotHub` (`Microsoft.Devices/iotHubs`). It's a synthetic parent with no operations of its own, created by the lowercase `@segment("iotHubs")` parent in [PrivateEndpointConnection.tsp](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/iothub/resource-manager/Microsoft.Devices/IoTHub/PrivateEndpointConnection.tsp#L41) and [GroupIdInformation.tsp](https://github.com/Azure/azure-rest-api-specs/blob/6e858cc12c77517bee29d619fcfe16e0e2d378d9/specification/iothub/resource-manager/Microsoft.Devices/IoTHub/GroupIdInformation.tsp#L43). The real resource, `IotHubDescription`, uses `Microsoft.Devices/IotHubs`. The rule only reports records that have their own GET or PUT, so it ignores this synthetic parent. Optional cleanup, not required by the rule: make the parent segment casing consistent, or reuse the `IotHubDescription` parent in those child operations.

## Coverage and limitations

All 670 normal candidate entrypoints were attempted, plus one supplemental graph. 644 normal candidates compiled without errors; 26 did not compile independently. The supplemental graph also had compilation errors and is not counted as clean. The table retains failures and explains whether each is an imported fragment covered in a successful program, a shared/configuration container without its own declared service, or an unresolved supplemental assessment. A failed standalone helper compile does not invalidate a successful enclosing service compile, nor does it count as a clean standalone service.

**Declaration-file reconciliation:** 649 files contain parsed service/provider namespace declarations. 1 declaration file was not reached by a successful program: the dormant NetApp fragment below. Its namespace, `Microsoft.NetApp`, is already covered by the normal NetApp service entrypoint. This is a source-fragment limitation, not an unassessed additional configured service.

**Supplemental NetApp limitation:** no normal entrypoint imports `Elastic/ElasticRegionInfoResource.tsp`. For a conditional check only, the compiler host added an import to NetApp's `main.tsp` in memory without changing its on-disk contents or line numbers. The augmented graph reported one resource duplicate, four `@typespec/http/duplicate-operation` errors (GET/list pairs) and two `@azure-tools/typespec-client-generator-core/duplicate-client-name` errors. These errors prevent treating the supplemental check as a successful assessment or an ordinary upstream-main violation; normal NetApp main compiled cleanly and reported zero rule violations.
